# v1.24.0 Re-scope Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Re-render the frozen exam in kubeagent v1.24.0's local verdict shape, port its three rule tables byte for byte, score the model's three remaining jobs, and sit the 0908 model through `kv-eval` only.

**Architecture:** Each story declares its objects (nodes, PVCs, registries). A pure Python port of kubeagent's rules turns those objects into the candidate list, the fresh-read lines and the "decided by rules" line, pinned by a golden fixture captured from a throwaway v1.24.0 worktree. Builders become transforms on declared objects. The scorer reads the row's meta and the reply, never re-derives, and scores job 1 (echo the decided cause), job 2 (name the cause on undecided rows) and job 3 (the summary agrees with the shared-cause label).

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.12, `dataclasses`, `pytest`, `ruff`; one throwaway Go worktree of kubeagent at `v1.24.0` (Go 1.26 at `/usr/local/go/bin`) for the capture only.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-14-v1240-rescope-design.md`

## Global Constraints

- **kubeagent is not changed.** Not one file, and `go.mod` stays as it
  is. The golden and the fixture are captured in a throwaway worktree
  that is removed afterwards.
- **No training, no export.** `kv-train` and `kv-export` are not run.
  Nothing writes under `dist/`. 0908 sits the exam through `kv-eval`
  only, and `kv-eval` writes only under `out/`.
- **Nothing runs on the training host.**
- **`kv-eval` is always given `--endpoint`.** The run has no
  `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` in the shell, and no key value is ever printed.
- **Every commit is `git commit -s`** under the repository's own git
  identity, the one every commit on `main` already carries. No AI
  attribution anywhere: not in commits, code, docs or the model card.
- **Never `git add -A`.** Name each file.
- **A commit message never cites a path under `docs/testing/`.**
- **No pin moves by a test flag.** The golden and the fixture are copied
  once from the section 7 capture. After that they change only by the
  re-pin steps in `PIN.md`.
- **No real identifier in any tracked file.** No real cluster, host,
  node, namespace, address, kubeconfig path or context name. Fixtures use
  `worker-1`..`worker-7`, `registry.invalid`, `mirror.invalid`,
  `registry.example.com` (the catalog's existing fake registry),
  `<ADDRESS>` and the names in `names.py`.
- **Simple voice in every doc line.** Short sentences, everyday words,
  the decision first, numbers explained.
- **TDD.** The failing test first, then the code. `pytest` and `ruff`
  pass on every commit, from the project's `.venv`.
- **The promise rule.** A comment, doc line or reason string that
  promises what the code does not keep is a defect. Close the gap or
  narrow the claim. Retraction is not deletion.
- **The eval must be able to fail 0908.** A "returns nothing" bot reads 0
  on all three jobs.

**Build order.** The plan sequences the work like this, because each
step pins the next: (1) `dataset/objects.py`; (2) the section 7 capture
in the throwaway worktree, which produces the golden and the fixture in
that shape; (3) `dataset/rules.py` against the fixture; (4) `contract.py`
and the system prompt against the golden; (5) `dataset/cases.py`;
(6) `evals/score.py`; (7) the smoke set; (8) the docs; (9) the run.

## Working rules for every task

- Repo: `/home/ubuntu/git/kubeagent-verdict`, branch `rescope-v1.24.0`
  (off `main` @ `ecfd67b`). Never commit on `main`.
- Tests: `.venv/bin/python -m pytest -q`. Lint: `.venv/bin/ruff check .`.
  Both green before every commit.
- Commit: `git add <named files>` then
  `git -c user.name=imantaba -c user.email=itn.taba@gmail.com commit -s -m "<msg>"`.
  No `Co-Authored-By`, no AI words in any message.
- Never run any test with `-update`. Never `kv-train`, never `kv-export`.
- Every new doc line: short sentence, plain words, the decision first.
- Build order step 5 is split into Task 5 (declarations) and Task 6
  (builders). Steps 6-9 are Tasks 7-10. The order is unchanged.

## Commit sequencing (why the golden is staged)

Landing the new golden at `contract/golden/` before the renderer knows
the fresh-read line would fail `test_user_message_matches_kubeagent_bytes`
and break "pytest passes on every commit". So:

- Task 2 commits the capture test text, `tests/fixtures/rules_golden.json`
  at its final path (no test reads it yet), and stages
  `user_message.txt`, `input.json`, `system_prompt.txt` under
  `contract/capture/out/`.
- Task 4 `git mv`s `user_message.txt` and `input.json` into
  `contract/golden/` with the renderer change. `render_candidates` prints
  the fresh-read line only when `fresh_read_outcome` is non-empty, which
  is exactly Go (a decision exists only for a candidate that was not
  ruled out), so every pre-existing row stays byte-identical and the two
  frozen hashes do not move in Task 4. `contract/golden/answer.json` has
  no producer in Task 2: Task 4 writes it by hand (one row per prompted
  workload — nine of the ten are decided by rules and echo the decided
  cause word for word; the tenth, `img/lone`, is not decided, and its
  row is authored separately) and checks it with
  `contract_check` against the captured input before it commits.
- Task 6 `git mv`s `system_prompt.txt` into `contract/system_prompt.txt`,
  swaps `contract.SYSTEM_PROMPT`, rewrites the builders, and re-pins
  `FROZEN_253_SHA256` and `EVAL_SET_SHA256` once. `_digest` hashes
  `generate.to_row(e)`, which carries the system message, so the swap
  and the builder rewrite move the hashes together, in one commit. Build
  order step 4's "and the system prompt" is met in Task 4 by a test that
  the staged file carries the inserted "decided by rules" paragraph.
- After Task 6 the directory `contract/capture/out/` is empty and gone.
  Only `contract/capture/kv_capture_test.go.txt` remains.

### Task 1: `dataset/objects.py`

**Files:**
- Create: `src/kubeagent_verdict/dataset/objects.py`
- Test: `tests/test_objects.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing from this branch.
- Produces (used by Tasks 3, 5, 6):
  - `Fresh(how="read", message="", ready="", unschedulable=False, extra=(), phase="", storage_class="", volume="", literal="", wrong_pod=False)` — frozen dataclass.
  - `Object(kind, name, scan_reason, placement, fresh, intent="decoy")` — frozen dataclass; `__post_init__` validates and raises `ValueError("object <kind>/<name>: …")`.
  - Tables: `KINDS`, `NODE_SCAN_REASONS`, `PVC_SCAN_REASONS`, `NODE_PLACEMENTS`, `PVC_PLACEMENTS`, `NODE_READY`, `FRESH_HOW`, `INTENTS`, `CONNECTION_LITERALS`, `AUTH_LITERALS`, `IMAGE_LITERALS`, `PULL_LITERALS`.
  - `validate(obj) -> None`, `refute(obj) -> Object`, `unverify(obj, how) -> Object`, `drop(objects, obj) -> tuple[Object, ...]`, `check_objects(entry_key, objects) -> None`.
  - Object names are templates (`"{node}"`, `"{pvc}"`) or literals (`registry.example.com`, `aux-0`); Task 6's `bind` formats them.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/test_objects.py`:

```python
import pytest

from kubeagent_verdict.dataset import objects as o


def node(**kw):
    base = {"kind": "node", "name": "{node}", "scan_reason": "NotReady",
            "placement": "on", "fresh": o.Fresh(ready="False")}
    base.update(kw)
    return o.Object(**base)


def pvc(**kw):
    base = {"kind": "pvc", "name": "{pvc}", "scan_reason": "ProvisioningFailed",
            "placement": "mounted",
            "fresh": o.Fresh(phase="Pending", storage_class="fast-ssd", volume="")}
    base.update(kw)
    return o.Object(**base)


def registry(**kw):
    base = {"kind": "registry", "name": "registry.example.com", "scan_reason": "2",
            "placement": "", "fresh": o.Fresh(literal="connection refused")}
    base.update(kw)
    return o.Object(**base)


def test_literal_tables_are_the_ones_decide_go_ships():
    assert o.CONNECTION_LITERALS[0] == "dial tcp"
    assert o.CONNECTION_LITERALS[-1] == "429 too many requests"
    assert len(o.CONNECTION_LITERALS) == 13
    assert o.AUTH_LITERALS == ("pull access denied", "no basic auth credentials",
                               "unauthorized", "denied")
    assert o.IMAGE_LITERALS == ("manifest unknown", "not found", "name unknown",
                                "repository does not exist", "invalid reference format")
    assert o.PULL_LITERALS == o.CONNECTION_LITERALS + o.AUTH_LITERALS + o.IMAGE_LITERALS


def test_valid_objects_construct():
    node()
    pvc()
    registry()
    node(fresh=o.Fresh(how="not_read"))
    node(fresh=o.Fresh(how="read_failed", message='nodes "{node}" is forbidden'))
    registry(fresh=o.Fresh(literal=""))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("bad, text", [
    ({"kind": "service"}, "object service/{node}: kind"),
    ({"scan_reason": "Broken"}, "object node/{node}: scan_reason"),
    ({"placement": "mounted"}, "object node/{node}: placement"),
    ({"fresh": o.Fresh(ready="Maybe")}, "object node/{node}: ready"),
    ({"fresh": o.Fresh(how="read_failed")}, "object node/{node}: read_failed needs a message"),
    ({"fresh": o.Fresh(how="later")}, "object node/{node}: how"),
    ({"intent": "joke"}, "object node/{node}: intent"),
    ({"fresh": o.Fresh(ready="True", wrong_pod=True)}, "object node/{node}: wrong_pod"),
])
def test_bad_node_values_name_the_object(bad, text):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError) as err:
        node(**bad)
    assert text in str(err.value)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("bad, text", [
    ({"scan_reason": "NotReady"}, "object pvc/{pvc}: scan_reason"),
    ({"placement": "on"}, "object pvc/{pvc}: placement"),
    ({"fresh": o.Fresh(phase="")}, "object pvc/{pvc}: phase"),
])
def test_bad_pvc_values_name_the_object(bad, text):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError) as err:
        pvc(**bad)
    assert text in str(err.value)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("bad, text", [
    ({"scan_reason": "two"}, "object registry/registry.example.com: scan_reason"),
    ({"placement": "on"}, "object registry/registry.example.com: placement"),
    ({"fresh": o.Fresh(literal="kaboom")}, "object registry/registry.example.com: literal"),
])
def test_bad_registry_values_name_the_object(bad, text):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError) as err:
        registry(**bad)
    assert text in str(err.value)


def test_refute_gives_each_kind_its_healthy_ending():
    n = o.refute(node(scan_reason="no kubelet lease", fresh=o.Fresh(how="read_failed", message="x")))
    assert (n.scan_reason, n.fresh.how, n.fresh.ready, n.fresh.message) == ("NotReady", "read", "True", "")
    p = o.refute(pvc(fresh=o.Fresh(phase="Pending", storage_class="fast-ssd", volume="pv-0821")))
    assert (p.fresh.phase, p.fresh.volume, p.fresh.storage_class) == ("Bound", "pv-0821", "fast-ssd")
    r = o.refute(registry(fresh=o.Fresh(literal="unauthorized", wrong_pod=True)))
    assert (r.fresh.literal, r.fresh.wrong_pod) == ("manifest unknown", False)


def test_unverify_read_failed_carries_the_name_template():
    assert o.unverify(node(), "read_failed").fresh == o.Fresh(
        how="read_failed", message='nodes "{node}" is forbidden')
    assert o.unverify(pvc(), "read_failed").fresh.message == \
        'persistentvolumeclaims "{pvc}" is forbidden'
    assert o.unverify(registry(), "read_failed").fresh.message == "events is forbidden"


def test_unverify_kind_specific_endings():
    n = o.unverify(node(), "lease")
    assert (n.scan_reason, n.fresh.ready) == ("no kubelet lease", "True")
    assert o.unverify(registry(), "auth").fresh.literal == "unauthorized"
    assert o.unverify(registry(), "no_event").fresh.literal == ""


@pytest.mark.parametrize("obj, how", [
    (node(), "auth"), (pvc(), "lease"), (registry(), "lease"), (node(), "nope"),
])
def test_unverify_rejects_the_wrong_combination(obj, how):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError) as err:
        o.unverify(obj, how)
    assert f"cannot unverify a {obj.kind} by {how!r}" in str(err.value)


def test_drop_removes_one_object_and_keeps_order():
    a, b, c = node(), pvc(), registry()
    assert o.drop((a, b, c), b) == (a, c)


def test_check_objects_rejects_duplicates_and_names_the_entry():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError) as err:
        o.check_objects("crashloop-pod", (node(), node()))
    assert str(err.value) == "entry crashloop-pod: object node/{node} is declared twice"
    o.check_objects("crashloop-pod", (node(), pvc(), registry()))


def test_check_objects_wraps_a_bad_object_with_the_entry_key():
    bad = o.Object.__new__(o.Object)
    object.__setattr__(bad, "kind", "node")
    object.__setattr__(bad, "name", "{node}")
    object.__setattr__(bad, "scan_reason", "Broken")
    object.__setattr__(bad, "placement", "on")
    object.__setattr__(bad, "fresh", o.Fresh(ready="True"))
    object.__setattr__(bad, "intent", "decoy")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError) as err:
        o.check_objects("crashloop-pod", (bad,))
    assert str(err.value).startswith("entry crashloop-pod: object node/{node}: scan_reason")
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `objects.py`**

Create `src/kubeagent_verdict/dataset/objects.py`:

```python
"""Declared objects: the nodes, PVCs and registries a story puts on the menu.

An Object says what kubeagent's scan saw (scan_reason, placement) and what
its fresh read finds (Fresh). rules.py turns a tuple of Objects into the
candidate list and the decision kubeagent v1.24.0 prints. Every value is
from a closed table; anything else raises ValueError naming the object.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, replace

KINDS = ("node", "pvc", "registry")
NODE_SCAN_REASONS = ("NotReady", "no kubelet lease", "kubelet not heartbeating")
PVC_SCAN_REASONS = (
    "ProvisioningFailed", "FailedBinding", "MissingStorageClass",
    "NoMatchingPV", "PVSelectorMismatch", "ProvisionerNotResponding",
)
NODE_PLACEMENTS = ("on", "off")
PVC_PLACEMENTS = ("mounted", "unmounted")
NODE_READY = ("True", "False", "Unknown", "missing")
FRESH_HOW = ("read", "read_failed", "not_read")
INTENTS = ("cause", "decoy")

# Copied verbatim from kubeagent internal/hypothesis/decide.go at v1.24.0.
CONNECTION_LITERALS = (
    "dial tcp", "i/o timeout", "connection refused", "connection reset",
    "no such host", "network is unreachable", "tls handshake", "x509:",
    "502 bad gateway", "503 service unavailable", "504 gateway timeout",
    "toomanyrequests", "429 too many requests",
)
AUTH_LITERALS = ("pull access denied", "no basic auth credentials", "unauthorized", "denied")
IMAGE_LITERALS = (
    "manifest unknown", "not found", "name unknown",
    "repository does not exist", "invalid reference format",
)
PULL_LITERALS = CONNECTION_LITERALS + AUTH_LITERALS + IMAGE_LITERALS


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Fresh:
    """What the fresh read finds. Only the fields for the object's kind are read."""

    how: str = "read"          # read | read_failed | not_read
    message: str = ""          # the failed-read message, when how == read_failed
    ready: str = ""            # node: True | False | Unknown | missing
    unschedulable: bool = False
    extra: tuple[str, ...] = ()  # node: extra condition/taint lines, gather format
    phase: str = ""            # pvc: Bound | Pending | Lost | anything else
    storage_class: str = ""
    volume: str = ""
    literal: str = ""          # registry: one of PULL_LITERALS, or "" for no event
    wrong_pod: bool = False    # registry: the events read hit a different pod


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Object:
    kind: str
    name: str
    scan_reason: str
    placement: str
    fresh: Fresh
    intent: str = "decoy"

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        validate(self)


def _err(obj: Object, what: str) -> ValueError:
    return ValueError(f"object {obj.kind}/{obj.name}: {what}")


def validate(obj: Object) -> None:
    f = obj.fresh
    if obj.kind not in KINDS:
        raise _err(obj, f"kind must be one of {KINDS}")
    if obj.intent not in INTENTS:
        raise _err(obj, f"intent must be one of {INTENTS}")
    if f.how not in FRESH_HOW:
        raise _err(obj, f"how must be one of {FRESH_HOW}")
    if f.how == "read_failed" and not f.message:
        raise _err(obj, "read_failed needs a message")
    if f.wrong_pod and obj.kind != "registry":
        raise _err(obj, "wrong_pod is only for a registry")
    if obj.kind == "node":
        if obj.scan_reason not in NODE_SCAN_REASONS:
            raise _err(obj, f"scan_reason must be one of {NODE_SCAN_REASONS}")
        if obj.placement not in NODE_PLACEMENTS:
            raise _err(obj, f"placement must be one of {NODE_PLACEMENTS}")
        if f.how == "read" and f.ready not in NODE_READY:
            raise _err(obj, f"ready must be one of {NODE_READY}")
    elif obj.kind == "pvc":
        if obj.scan_reason not in PVC_SCAN_REASONS:
            raise _err(obj, f"scan_reason must be one of {PVC_SCAN_REASONS}")
        if obj.placement not in PVC_PLACEMENTS:
            raise _err(obj, f"placement must be one of {PVC_PLACEMENTS}")
        if f.how == "read" and not f.phase:
            raise _err(obj, "phase must be set on a read PVC")
    else:
        if not obj.scan_reason.isdigit():
            raise _err(obj, "scan_reason must be the count of workloads failing to pull")
        if obj.placement != "":
            raise _err(obj, "placement must be empty on a registry")
        if f.how == "read" and f.literal and f.literal not in PULL_LITERALS:
            raise _err(obj, "literal must be one of the pull literals or empty")


def refute(obj: Object) -> Object:
    """The healthy ending: the fresh read says the object is fine."""
    if obj.kind == "node":
        return replace(obj, scan_reason="NotReady",
                       fresh=replace(obj.fresh, how="read", message="", ready="True"))
    if obj.kind == "pvc":
        return replace(obj, fresh=replace(obj.fresh, how="read", message="", phase="Bound"))
    return replace(obj, fresh=replace(obj.fresh, how="read", message="",
                                      literal="manifest unknown", wrong_pod=False))


def unverify(obj: Object, how: str) -> Object:
    """An ending the rules cannot settle: the object stays decided but unverified."""
    if how == "read_failed":
        message = {
            "node": f'nodes "{obj.name}" is forbidden',
            "pvc": f'persistentvolumeclaims "{obj.name}" is forbidden',
            "registry": "events is forbidden",
        }[obj.kind]
        return replace(obj, fresh=Fresh(how="read_failed", message=message))
    if obj.kind == "node" and how == "lease":
        return replace(obj, scan_reason="no kubelet lease",
                       fresh=replace(obj.fresh, how="read", message="", ready="True"))
    if obj.kind == "registry" and how == "auth":
        return replace(obj, fresh=replace(obj.fresh, how="read", message="",
                                          literal="unauthorized", wrong_pod=False))
    if obj.kind == "registry" and how == "no_event":
        return replace(obj, fresh=replace(obj.fresh, how="read", message="",
                                          literal="", wrong_pod=False))
    raise _err(obj, f"cannot unverify a {obj.kind} by {how!r}")


def drop(objects: tuple[Object, ...], obj: Object) -> tuple[Object, ...]:
    return tuple(o for o in objects if o != obj)


def check_objects(entry_key: str, objects: tuple[Object, ...]) -> None:
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for obj in objects:
        try:
            validate(obj)
        except ValueError as err:
            raise ValueError(f"entry {entry_key}: {err}") from None
        key = f"{obj.kind}/{obj.name}"
        if key in seen:
            raise ValueError(f"entry {entry_key}: object {key} is declared twice")
        seen.add(key)
```

### Task 2: The v1.24.0 capture (golden + rules fixture)

**Files:**
- Create: `contract/capture/kv_capture_test.go.txt` (the Go test, kept as text; never lands in kubeagent)
- Create: `tests/fixtures/rules_golden.json` (copied from the capture)
- Create: `contract/capture/out/user_message.txt`, `contract/capture/out/input.json`, `contract/capture/out/system_prompt.txt` (staged; moved in Tasks 4 and 6)
- Modify: `contract/PIN.md` (append the capture record)
- Test: `tests/test_capture_staging.py` (new, small; deleted in Task 6 when the staging directory goes)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: kubeagent at tag `v1.24.0` = commit `15ec5649bbd2d07558eae945b71430afc8f231fd`, in a throwaway worktree. Go at `/usr/local/go/bin`. Nothing from Task 1.
- Produces:
  - `tests/fixtures/rules_golden.json` with the shape below (Task 3 reads it).
  - `contract/capture/out/input.json`: today's keys plus per candidate `fresh_read_outcome`, `fresh_read_evidence` and per workload `decided`, `decided_cause`, `decided_outcome` (Task 4 reads it).
  - `contract/capture/out/user_message.txt`: the v1.24.0 prompt bytes for that input (Task 4).
  - `contract/capture/out/system_prompt.txt`: `verdictSystemPrompt` bytes (Task 6).

**Fixture design.** Twelve flagged workloads, six down nodes, six PVC
issues in `db`, one registry with five pullers plus one lone puller.
The fixture keeps all twelve so every `check` branch is pinned; the
prompt shows the first ten (the gather's cap). The bundle shows eight
reads; `Reads` holds every read the branches need. Those are two pins,
kept apart on purpose.

| # | workload | issue | pod node | what it pins |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `web/frontend` | ImagePullBackOff (`registry.invalid`) | worker-1, worker-3, worker-5, worker-6, worker-7 | node attributed + Ready False → confirmed; outranked nodes: never read, Ready Unknown, no Ready condition, read failed; registry outranked + image literal → refuted |
| 2 | `db/postgres` | Pending | worker-4 (healthy) | 6 nodes ruled out; PVCs `aux-0` Bound → refuted, `aux-1` read failed, `aux-2` phase `Released` → unverified, `aux-3`/`aux-4` unmounted, `data-0` Lost → confirmed, decided line names the 12th candidate, past the 8-cap |
| 3 | `db/cache` | Pending | worker-2 | node `no kubelet lease` + Ready True → unverified; PVC `cache-0` `ProvisionerNotResponding` class `standard`, Pending → confirmed; decided = the PVC (a confirmed beats an earlier unverified) |
| 4 | `db/search` | Pending | worker-4 | PVC `search-0` same class → confirmed; storage-class group of 2 |
| 5 | `img/one` | ImagePullBackOff | worker-4 | registry attributed (5 pullers); `connection refused` → confirmed |
| 6 | `img/two` | ErrImagePull | worker-4 | `i/o timeout` → confirmed; registry group of 2 |
| 7 | `img/three` | ImagePullBackOff | worker-4 | `unauthorized` → unverified |
| 8 | `img/five` | ImagePullBackOff | worker-4 | events read, none → `no pull event names the failure; events may have aged out` |
| 9 | `img/six` | CrashLoopBackOff on pod A then ImagePullBackOff on pod B | worker-4 | events read for pod A only → `events of the pulling pod were not read` |
| 10 | `img/lone` | ImagePullBackOff (`mirror.invalid`) | worker-4 | registry ruled out (1 puller); zero decisions; undecided |
| 11 | `img/seven` | ImagePullBackOff | worker-4 | events read failed → `fresh read failed: events is forbidden` (fixture only) |
| 12 | `img/eight` | ImagePullBackOff | worker-4 | nothing read → `not re-read: the read budget was spent first` (fixture only) |

Down nodes: `worker-1 NotReady`, `worker-2 no kubelet lease`,
`worker-3 kubelet not heartbeating`, `worker-5 NotReady`, `worker-6 NotReady`,
`worker-7 NotReady`. Every flagged workload gets all six as candidates,
ruled out where no pod of it sits on the node (checker fact 1).

Expected `shared` (three lines, most workloads first, then key order):
`2 workloads share one upstream cause: registry registry.invalid (7 workloads failing to pull)`,
`2 workloads share one upstream cause: storage class standard (ProvisionerNotResponding)`.
(`node/worker-1` has one confirmed workload, so no node line.) If the
capture prints them in another order, the fixture is right and this
table is wrong: the fixture is the pin.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the staging test (fails until the files exist)**

Create `tests/test_capture_staging.py`:

```python
import json
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
OUT = REPO / "contract" / "capture" / "out"
FIXTURE = REPO / "tests" / "fixtures" / "rules_golden.json"


def test_capture_files_are_staged():
    for name in ("user_message.txt", "input.json", "system_prompt.txt"):
        assert (OUT / name).is_file(), name


def test_staged_input_carries_the_new_fields():
    d = json.loads((OUT / "input.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    decided = [w for w in d["workloads"] if w["decided"]]
    assert decided, "at least one workload is decided by rules"
    for w in d["workloads"]:
        assert set(w) >= {"decided", "decided_cause", "decided_outcome", "candidates"}
        for cd in w["candidates"]:
            assert set(cd) == {"cause", "verdict", "reason",
                               "fresh_read_outcome", "fresh_read_evidence"}
            if cd["verdict"] == "ruled_out":
                assert cd["fresh_read_outcome"] == "" and cd["fresh_read_evidence"] == ""


def test_staged_prompt_has_the_three_new_shapes():
    text = (OUT / "user_message.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "      fresh read: confirmed — " in text
    assert "    decided by rules: " in text
    assert "    [truncated by kubeagent]\n" in text


def test_staged_system_prompt_has_the_decided_paragraph():
    text = (OUT / "system_prompt.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert 'A workload marked "decided by rules" has its cause fixed by kubeagent' in text


def test_rules_fixture_shape():
    d = json.loads(FIXTURE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert d["kubeagent"] == {
        "tag": "v1.24.0", "commit": "15ec5649bbd2d07558eae945b71430afc8f231fd"}
    assert len(d["workloads"]) == 12
    assert len(d["reads"]) == 8
    assert len(d["shared"]) == 2
    for w in d["workloads"]:
        assert set(w) == {"namespace", "name", "pod", "issue", "image",
                          "candidates", "result"}
        assert set(w["result"]) == {"decided", "cause", "outcome", "evidence",
                                    "group_key", "group_text", "decisions"}


def test_no_real_identifier_in_capture_outputs():
    banned = ("kubeconfig", "/home/", "@", "kubeagent-live", "http://", "https://")
    for path in (OUT / "user_message.txt", OUT / "input.json", FIXTURE):
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for b in banned:
            assert b not in text, f"{path.name} contains {b!r}"
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the capture test text**

Create `contract/capture/kv_capture_test.go.txt`. The four helpers
`goldenCluster`, `goldenSummary`, `goldenServiceIssues`, `goldenPlatformLine`
and the finding-to-JSON block are the same fixture values the old capture
template used (`/home/ubuntu/git/kubeagent/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-22-kubeagent-verdict.md`
lines 846-949; that template produced today's `contract/golden/input.json`).
Copy those values into the helpers so the cluster, resource and service
lines of the prompt keep today's bytes; the JSON keys for `cluster`,
`summary`, `platform_line`, `service_issues` and each finding must equal
the keys in today's `contract/golden/input.json` exactly. Everything
else below is new.

```go
//go:build goldencapture

// Golden capture for kubeagent-verdict. This file lives in
// kubeagent-verdict as contract/capture/kv_capture_test.go.txt and is
// copied into a throwaway worktree of kubeagent to run. It never lands
// in kubeagent.
//
// The five steps, from /home/ubuntu/git/kubeagent (HEAD at tag v1.24.0):
//   1. git worktree add "$SCRATCH/ka-v1.24.0" v1.24.0
//   2. cp /home/ubuntu/git/kubeagent-verdict/contract/capture/kv_capture_test.go.txt \
//         "$SCRATCH/ka-v1.24.0/internal/investigate/kv_capture_test.go"
//   3. cd "$SCRATCH/ka-v1.24.0" && export PATH=$PATH:/usr/local/go/bin && \
//      KV_GOLDEN_DIR="$SCRATCH/capture" go test -tags goldencapture \
//         -run TestCaptureVerdictGolden ./internal/investigate -v
//   4. copy rules_golden.json to tests/fixtures/ and the other three files
//      to contract/capture/out/ in kubeagent-verdict; record the tag and
//      commit in contract/PIN.md
//   5. git worktree remove --force "$SCRATCH/ka-v1.24.0" && \
//      git worktree prune && git status --short   # must print nothing
//
// Re-run these five steps only by the re-pin procedure in contract/PIN.md.

package investigate

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	corev1 "k8s.io/api/core/v1"
	metav1 "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/apis/meta/v1"

	"github.com/imantaba/kubeagent/internal/clusterhealth"
	"github.com/imantaba/kubeagent/internal/diagnose"
	"github.com/imantaba/kubeagent/internal/hypothesis"
	"github.com/imantaba/kubeagent/internal/inventory"
	"github.com/imantaba/kubeagent/internal/pvchealth"
	"github.com/imantaba/kubeagent/internal/rootcause"
)

const (
	kvTag    = "v1.24.0"
	kvCommit = "15ec5649bbd2d07558eae945b71430afc8f231fd"
	regHost  = "registry.invalid"
)

// Pod names are fixed so the prompt and the fixture are reproducible.
var pods = map[string]string{
	"web/frontend": "frontend-5b8d7f6c9-q2w3e",
	"db/postgres":  "postgres-0",
	"db/cache":     "cache-0",
	"db/search":    "search-0",
	"img/one":      "one-7c9d4b5f6-abcde",
	"img/two":      "two-7c9d4b5f6-bcdef",
	"img/three":    "three-7c9d4b5f6-cdefg",
	"img/five":     "five-7c9d4b5f6-defgh",
	"img/six-a":    "six-6f8e9d0a1-crash",
	"img/six-b":    "six-6f8e9d0a1-pulls",
	"img/lone":     "lone-7c9d4b5f6-efghi",
	"img/seven":    "seven-7c9d4b5f6-fghij",
	"img/eight":    "eight-7c9d4b5f6-ghijk",
}

func pullFinding(ns, pod, issue, image string) diagnose.Finding {
	return diagnose.Finding{
		Pod:      ns + "/" + pod,
		Issue:    issue,
		Reason:   "image pull keeps failing",
		Evidence: `Failed to pull image "` + image + `"`,
		Image:    image,
	}
}

func pendingFinding(ns, pod string) diagnose.Finding {
	return diagnose.Finding{
		Pod:      ns + "/" + pod,
		Issue:    "Pending",
		Reason:   "pod is not scheduled",
		Evidence: "0/7 nodes are available: pod has unbound immediate PersistentVolumeClaims",
	}
}

func crashFinding(ns, pod string) diagnose.Finding {
	return diagnose.Finding{
		Pod:      ns + "/" + pod,
		Issue:    "CrashLoopBackOff",
		Reason:   "container keeps exiting",
		Evidence: "back-off 5m0s restarting failed container",
	}
}

func puller(ns, name, issue, node string) inventory.Workload {
	image := regHost + "/" + ns + "/" + name + ":1.0"
	pod := pods[ns+"/"+name]
	return inventory.Workload{
		Namespace: ns, Name: name, Kind: "Deployment",
		Ready: 0, Desired: 1, Status: "Progressing",
		Findings: []diagnose.Finding{pullFinding(ns, pod, issue, image)},
		Pods:     []inventory.PodRow{{Name: pod, Node: node}},
	}
}

func captureWorkloads() []inventory.Workload {
	frontendImage := regHost + "/web/frontend:2.1"
	frontendPods := []inventory.PodRow{
		{Name: pods["web/frontend"], Node: "worker-1"},
		{Name: "frontend-5b8d7f6c9-r4t5y", Node: "worker-3"},
		{Name: "frontend-5b8d7f6c9-u6i7o", Node: "worker-5"},
		{Name: "frontend-5b8d7f6c9-p8a9s", Node: "worker-6"},
		{Name: "frontend-5b8d7f6c9-d0f1g", Node: "worker-7"},
	}
	six := inventory.Workload{
		Namespace: "img", Name: "six", Kind: "Deployment",
		Ready: 0, Desired: 2, Status: "Progressing",
		Findings: []diagnose.Finding{
			crashFinding("img", pods["img/six-a"]),
			pullFinding("img", pods["img/six-b"], "ImagePullBackOff", regHost+"/img/six:1.0"),
		},
		Pods: []inventory.PodRow{
			{Name: pods["img/six-a"], Node: "worker-4"},
			{Name: pods["img/six-b"], Node: "worker-4"},
		},
	}
	lone := puller("img", "lone", "ImagePullBackOff", "worker-4")
	lone.Findings[0].Image = "mirror.invalid/img/lone:1.0"
	lone.Findings[0].Evidence = `Failed to pull image "mirror.invalid/img/lone:1.0"`
	return []inventory.Workload{
		{
			Namespace: "web", Name: "frontend", Kind: "Deployment",
			Ready: 0, Desired: 5, Status: "Progressing",
			Findings: []diagnose.Finding{pullFinding("web", pods["web/frontend"], "ImagePullBackOff", frontendImage)},
			Pods:     frontendPods,
		},
		{
			Namespace: "db", Name: "postgres", Kind: "StatefulSet",
			Ready: 0, Desired: 1, Status: "Progressing",
			Findings: []diagnose.Finding{pendingFinding("db", pods["db/postgres"])},
			Pods:     []inventory.PodRow{{Name: pods["db/postgres"], Node: "worker-4"}},
		},
		{
			Namespace: "db", Name: "cache", Kind: "StatefulSet",
			Ready: 0, Desired: 1, Status: "Progressing",
			Findings: []diagnose.Finding{pendingFinding("db", pods["db/cache"])},
			Pods:     []inventory.PodRow{{Name: pods["db/cache"], Node: "worker-2"}},
		},
		{
			Namespace: "db", Name: "search", Kind: "StatefulSet",
			Ready: 0, Desired: 1, Status: "Progressing",
			Findings: []diagnose.Finding{pendingFinding("db", pods["db/search"])},
			Pods:     []inventory.PodRow{{Name: pods["db/search"], Node: "worker-4"}},
		},
		puller("img", "one", "ImagePullBackOff", "worker-4"),
		puller("img", "two", "ErrImagePull", "worker-4"),
		puller("img", "three", "ImagePullBackOff", "worker-4"),
		puller("img", "five", "ImagePullBackOff", "worker-4"),
		six,
		lone,
		puller("img", "seven", "ImagePullBackOff", "worker-4"),
		puller("img", "eight", "ImagePullBackOff", "worker-4"),
	}
}

func capturePodPVCs() map[string][]string {
	return map[string][]string{
		"db/" + pods["db/postgres"]: {"aux-0", "aux-1", "aux-2", "data-0"},
		"db/" + pods["db/cache"]:    {"cache-0"},
		"db/" + pods["db/search"]:   {"search-0"},
	}
}

func capturePVCIssues() []pvchealth.Issue {
	issue := func(name, reason, class string) pvchealth.Issue {
		return pvchealth.Issue{Namespace: "db", Name: name, Phase: "Pending",
			Reason: reason, Detail: "waiting for a volume", StorageClass: class}
	}
	return []pvchealth.Issue{
		issue("aux-0", "ProvisioningFailed", "fast-ssd"),
		issue("aux-1", "ProvisioningFailed", "fast-ssd"),
		issue("aux-2", "ProvisioningFailed", "fast-ssd"),
		issue("aux-3", "ProvisioningFailed", "fast-ssd"),
		issue("aux-4", "ProvisioningFailed", "fast-ssd"),
		issue("data-0", "FailedBinding", "fast-ssd"),
		issue("cache-0", "ProvisionerNotResponding", "standard"),
		issue("search-0", "ProvisionerNotResponding", "standard"),
	}
}

func nodeWith(name string, unschedulable bool, conds []corev1.NodeCondition, taints []corev1.Taint) *corev1.Node {
	n := &corev1.Node{ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{Name: name}}
	n.Spec.Unschedulable = unschedulable
	n.Spec.Taints = taints
	n.Status.Conditions = conds
	return n
}

func ready(status corev1.ConditionStatus, reason, msg string) corev1.NodeCondition {
	return corev1.NodeCondition{Type: corev1.NodeReady, Status: status, Reason: reason, Message: msg}
}

func pvcWith(name string, phase corev1.PersistentVolumeClaimPhase, class, volume string) *corev1.PersistentVolumeClaim {
	p := &corev1.PersistentVolumeClaim{ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{Namespace: "db", Name: name}}
	p.Spec.VolumeName = volume
	if class != "" {
		c := class
		p.Spec.StorageClassName = &c
	}
	p.Status.Phase = phase
	return p
}

func pullEvent(image, tail string) corev1.Event {
	return corev1.Event{Reason: "Failed", Count: 4,
		Message: `Failed to pull image "` + image + `": ` + tail}
}

func captureReads() hypothesis.Reads {
	notReadyTaint := []corev1.Taint{{Key: "node.kubernetes.io/not-ready", Effect: corev1.TaintEffectNoSchedule}}
	return hypothesis.Reads{
		Nodes: map[string]*corev1.Node{
			"worker-1": nodeWith("worker-1", false,
				[]corev1.NodeCondition{ready(corev1.ConditionFalse, "KubeletNotReady", "container runtime is down")}, notReadyTaint),
			"worker-2": nodeWith("worker-2", false,
				[]corev1.NodeCondition{ready(corev1.ConditionTrue, "KubeletReady", "kubelet is posting ready status")}, nil),
			"worker-5": nodeWith("worker-5", false,
				[]corev1.NodeCondition{ready(corev1.ConditionUnknown, "NodeStatusUnknown", "Kubelet stopped posting node status.")}, nil),
			"worker-6": nodeWith("worker-6", false, nil, nil),
		},
		PVCs: map[string]*corev1.PersistentVolumeClaim{
			"db/aux-0":    pvcWith("aux-0", corev1.ClaimBound, "fast-ssd", "pv-0442"),
			"db/aux-2":    pvcWith("aux-2", corev1.PersistentVolumeClaimPhase("Released"), "fast-ssd", ""),
			"db/data-0":   pvcWith("data-0", corev1.ClaimLost, "fast-ssd", "pv-0821"),
			"db/cache-0":  pvcWith("cache-0", corev1.ClaimPending, "standard", ""),
			"db/search-0": pvcWith("search-0", corev1.ClaimPending, "standard", ""),
		},
		Events: map[string][]corev1.Event{
			"img/" + pods["img/one"]:   {pullEvent(regHost+"/img/one:1.0", "connection refused")},
			"img/" + pods["img/two"]:   {pullEvent(regHost+"/img/two:1.0", "i/o timeout")},
			"img/" + pods["img/three"]: {pullEvent(regHost+"/img/three:1.0", "unauthorized: authentication required")},
			"img/" + pods["img/five"]:  {},
			"img/" + pods["img/six-a"]: {{Reason: "BackOff", Count: 9, Message: "Back-off restarting failed container"}},
			"web/" + pods["web/frontend"]: {pullEvent(regHost+"/web/frontend:2.1", "manifest unknown")},
		},
		Failed: map[string]string{
			"node/worker-7":              `nodes "worker-7" is forbidden`,
			"pvc/db/aux-1":               `persistentvolumeclaims "aux-1" is forbidden`,
			"events/img/" + pods["img/seven"]: "events is forbidden",
		},
	}
}

type readJSON struct {
	Label   string `json:"label"`
	Content string `json:"content"`
}

// captureBundle is the eight reads the prompt shows, in gather order:
// nodes, then PVCs, then events. describeNode, describePVC and
// formatEvents live in reader.go (same package; reader.go:238, :250, :312
// at v1.24.0); open it for their exact signatures and call them with the
// objects captureReads holds so the content bytes are the gather's own.
func captureBundle(reads hypothesis.Reads) []readJSON {
	return []readJSON{
		{"describe node /worker-1", describeNode(reads.Nodes["worker-1"])},
		{"describe node /worker-2", describeNode(reads.Nodes["worker-2"])},
		{"describe pvc db/aux-0", describePVC(reads.PVCs["db/aux-0"])},
		{"describe pvc db/data-0", describePVC(reads.PVCs["db/data-0"])},
		{"describe pvc db/cache-0", describePVC(reads.PVCs["db/cache-0"])},
		{"events img/" + pods["img/one"], formatEvents("img", pods["img/one"], reads.Events["img/"+pods["img/one"]])},
		{"events img/" + pods["img/five"], formatEvents("img", pods["img/five"], nil)},
		{"describe node /worker-7", "read failed: " + reads.Failed["node/worker-7"]},
	}
}

type candidateJSON struct {
	Cause             string `json:"cause"`
	Verdict           string `json:"verdict"`
	Reason            string `json:"reason"`
	FreshReadOutcome  string `json:"fresh_read_outcome"`
	FreshReadEvidence string `json:"fresh_read_evidence"`
}

type decisionJSON struct {
	Candidate string `json:"candidate"`
	Outcome   string `json:"outcome"`
	Evidence  string `json:"evidence"`
}

type resultJSON struct {
	Decided   bool           `json:"decided"`
	Cause     string         `json:"cause"`
	Outcome   string         `json:"outcome"`
	Evidence  string         `json:"evidence"`
	GroupKey  string         `json:"group_key"`
	GroupText string         `json:"group_text"`
	Decisions []decisionJSON `json:"decisions"`
}

type fixtureWorkloadJSON struct {
	Namespace  string          `json:"namespace"`
	Name       string          `json:"name"`
	Pod        string          `json:"pod"`
	Issue      string          `json:"issue"`
	Image      string          `json:"image"`
	Candidates []candidateJSON `json:"candidates"`
	Result     resultJSON      `json:"result"`
}

type fixtureJSON struct {
	Kubeagent map[string]string     `json:"kubeagent"`
	Workloads []fixtureWorkloadJSON `json:"workloads"`
	Shared    []string              `json:"shared"`
	Reads     []readJSON            `json:"reads"`
}

func candidatesJSON(w inventory.Workload, r hypothesis.Result) []candidateJSON {
	out := make([]candidateJSON, 0, len(w.RootCauseTrace))
	for _, h := range w.RootCauseTrace {
		c := candidateJSON{Cause: h.Cause, Verdict: string(h.Verdict), Reason: h.Reason}
		for _, d := range r.Decisions {
			if d.Candidate.Cause == h.Cause {
				c.FreshReadOutcome, c.FreshReadEvidence = string(d.Outcome), d.Evidence
			}
		}
		out = append(out, c)
	}
	return out
}

func resultToJSON(r hypothesis.Result) resultJSON {
	out := resultJSON{Decided: r.Decided, Cause: r.Cause, Outcome: string(r.Outcome),
		Evidence: r.Evidence, GroupKey: r.GroupKey, GroupText: r.GroupText,
		Decisions: []decisionJSON{}}
	for _, d := range r.Decisions {
		out.Decisions = append(out.Decisions, decisionJSON{d.Candidate.Cause, string(d.Outcome), d.Evidence})
	}
	return out
}

func firstPod(w inventory.Workload) (pod, issue, image string) {
	f := w.Findings[0]
	if i := strings.IndexByte(f.Pod, '/'); i >= 0 {
		pod = f.Pod[i+1:]
	}
	return pod, f.Issue, f.Image
}

func writeFile(t *testing.T, dir, name string, data []byte) {
	t.Helper()
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, name), data, 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func TestCaptureVerdictGolden(t *testing.T) {
	dir := os.Getenv("KV_GOLDEN_DIR")
	if dir == "" {
		t.Skip("KV_GOLDEN_DIR not set")
	}
	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o755); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	wl := captureWorkloads()
	down := []clusterhealth.DownNode{
		{Name: "worker-1", Reason: "NotReady"},
		{Name: "worker-2", Reason: "no kubelet lease"},
		{Name: "worker-3", Reason: "kubelet not heartbeating"},
		{Name: "worker-5", Reason: "NotReady"},
		{Name: "worker-6", Reason: "NotReady"},
		{Name: "worker-7", Reason: "NotReady"},
	}
	rootcause.Annotate(wl, down)
	rootcause.AnnotatePVC(wl, capturePodPVCs(), capturePVCIssues())
	rootcause.AnnotateRegistry(wl)

	reads := captureReads()
	results := make([]hypothesis.Result, len(wl))
	for i := range wl {
		results[i] = hypothesis.Decide(wl[i], reads)
	}
	shared := hypothesis.Shared(results)

	// The prompt shows the first ten workloads: the gather's cap.
	scoped, scopedResults := wl[:10], results[:10]
	bundle := captureBundle(reads)
	var b strings.Builder
	var trail []string
	spent := 0
	for _, r := range bundle {
		appendRead(&b, &trail, &spent, r.Label, r.Content)
	}
	cluster := goldenCluster()
	summary := goldenSummary()
	issues := goldenServiceIssues()
	prompt := buildVerdictPrompt(cluster, summary, nil, issues, scoped, scopedResults, b.String())
	writeFile(t, dir, "user_message.txt", []byte(prompt))
	writeFile(t, dir, "system_prompt.txt", []byte(verdictSystemPrompt))

	// input.json: today's keys, plus the five new ones. The cluster,
	// summary, platform_line, service_issues and findings blocks use the
	// same conversion the old template used (see the note above the code).
	type workloadJSON struct {
		Namespace      string            `json:"namespace"`
		Name           string            `json:"name"`
		Kind           string            `json:"kind"`
		Ready          int               `json:"ready"`
		Desired        int               `json:"desired"`
		Status         string            `json:"status"`
		Restarts       int               `json:"restarts"`
		Confidence     string            `json:"confidence"`
		Findings       []map[string]any  `json:"findings"`
		Candidates     []candidateJSON   `json:"candidates"`
		Decided        bool              `json:"decided"`
		DecidedCause   string            `json:"decided_cause"`
		DecidedOutcome string            `json:"decided_outcome"`
	}
	input := map[string]any{
		"cluster":        clusterJSON(cluster),
		"summary":        summaryJSON(summary),
		"platform_line":  goldenPlatformLine(),
		"service_issues": serviceIssuesJSON(issues),
		"reads":          bundle,
	}
	var wj []workloadJSON
	for i, w := range scoped {
		r := scopedResults[i]
		wj = append(wj, workloadJSON{
			Namespace: w.Namespace, Name: w.Name, Kind: w.Kind,
			Ready: int(w.Ready), Desired: int(w.Desired), Status: w.Status,
			Restarts: int(w.Restarts), Confidence: w.RootCauseConfidence,
			Findings: findingsJSON(w.Findings), Candidates: candidatesJSON(w, r),
			Decided: r.Decided, DecidedCause: r.Cause, DecidedOutcome: string(r.Outcome),
		})
	}
	input["workloads"] = wj
	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(input, "", "  ")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	writeFile(t, dir, "input.json", append(data, '\n'))

	fx := fixtureJSON{
		Kubeagent: map[string]string{"tag": kvTag, "commit": kvCommit},
		Shared:    shared, Reads: bundle,
	}
	if fx.Shared == nil {
		fx.Shared = []string{}
	}
	for i, w := range wl {
		pod, issue, image := firstPod(w)
		fx.Workloads = append(fx.Workloads, fixtureWorkloadJSON{
			Namespace: w.Namespace, Name: w.Name, Pod: pod, Issue: issue, Image: image,
			Candidates: candidatesJSON(w, results[i]), Result: resultToJSON(results[i]),
		})
	}
	data, err = json.MarshalIndent(fx, "", "  ")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	writeFile(t, dir, "rules_golden.json", append(data, '\n'))
	t.Logf("wrote 4 files under %s; %d workloads in the fixture, %d in the prompt, %d shared lines",
		dir, len(wl), len(scoped), len(shared))
}
```

Notes for the implementer, in order of likelihood:
- `go vet` naming a field (`inventory.Workload`, `inventory.PodRow`,
  `diagnose.Finding`, `pvchealth.Issue`, `clusterhealth.DownNode`,
  `hypothesis.Reads`) means the fixture guessed a name; open that type in
  the worktree and fix the fixture. Never touch production code.
- `describeNode`, `describePVC`, `formatEvents` are in `reader.go`
  (`describeNode(n *corev1.Node) string`, `describePVC(p *corev1.PersistentVolumeClaim) string`,
  `formatEvents(namespace, name string, items []corev1.Event) string`). If a
  signature differs (an extra namespace or pod argument, a different
  return), adapt the call in `captureBundle` only.
- `firstPod` uses the first finding on purpose: that is `eventsPod(w)`.
  For `img/six` the fixture's `pod` is the crash pod, and Python's
  `Object` for its registry carries `wrong_pod=True`.
- If the six-node fan-out makes the prompt exceed 64 KiB, `buildVerdictPrompt`
  caps it and the byte pin still holds; nothing to change.
